# Weekly delta 2026-W40
Window 2026-09-28 to 2026-10-05 (UTC): the agents made 101 card decisions, but no belief moved and no experiment ran, because nothing can execute experiments yet [metrics.json].

## For the CTO
- What we believe: unchanged. No belief was revised this week, and no test ran that could change one [metrics.json].
- Waiting for you: one card has two answers. You approved it, and the research agent re-recommended it 19 seconds earlier; the record holds it at "recommended" until someone settles it [IDX-20261005T025230Z].
- Dropped: the research agent turned down 14 ideas, mostly "cannot run this quarter" or "lower priority", and folded 7 into tests already planned [IDX-20261005T025230Z].
- Broke: both news feeds produced daily digests for only 3 of 7 days, three evidence requests are past due with no answer, and 436 records point at sources not on file [metrics.json].
- Cost: 680 paid API calls this week. Nobody has set a budget, so nobody can say whether that is high [metrics.json].

  - A Jev skill router fixed 37 requests and broke 7 that were already correct (488 requests) [feed:memory 2026-09-29].

## 6. Landscape diff
- Decision models in production:
  - Streak shipped a Jev prompt classifier: median latency 1.5 s to 200 ms, core feature metric +74% [feed:memory 2026-09-29 https://x.com/aloo/status/2104959020616597745].
  - Vega (second-hand vendor numbers): about a third of security alerts cleared by Jev at 98% correct; Vercel says about 13% of paid AI Gateway teams used Jev on day one [feed:memory 2026-09-30].
  - At 200,000 events, Jev decisions cost $11.76 vs $240 for writing (author-reported) [feed:localai 2026-09-29 https://x.com/gippp69/status/2104894644278497751].
  - JevRouter (Straitly) launched, claiming 99% of frontier quality at 40% of cost, no misroute rate [feed:localai 2026-09-28].
- Open replicas of the decision tier:
  - OpenDecider od1 (4B) reports 79.65% on typed decisions; train/test template overlap flagged, no calibration figures [feed:localai 2026-09-28].
  - Julia-1 (144M, Apache 2.0, CPU) reports 73.15% vs Jev's 72.70% on Typed Decisions [feed:memory 2026-09-28 https://x.com/aibuilderclub_/status/2104415947260088450].
  - RSI-Jev (2B): 1 of 59 RL runs helped, ranking the right memory first 60% more often [feed:memory 2026-09-28 https://x.com/GaoShanghua/status/2104610851197985207].
- Memory graded at the solver:
  - MemProbe: Mem0 attributes a remembered value's origin correctly 17.7% of the time, A-MEM and Cognee 64.5% [feed:memory 2026-09-29 https://x.com/vigram_void/status/2104962528573018309].
  - JAM (arXiv 2609.34385): LongMemEval 65.2% vs 56.2% for RAG on the same 4B model, at higher latency [feed:memory 2026-09-30].
  - Caura open-sourced (MIT) a LoCoMo runner with full-context and random controls: 87.27% for about $3 [feed:memory 2026-09-30].
- Provenance as memory's trust boundary:
  - Backchain Memory Attack: provenance-bound authorization cuts attack success 11.0% to 2.0% at 92.1% legitimate success [feed:memory 2026-09-29 https://x.com/gastronomy/status/2104751701345304745].
  - One poisoned shared file reached 60 to 100% of 30 persistent-memory assistants (arXiv 2609.35576) [feed:memory 2026-09-29 https://x.com/KostyaAI/status/2104865418573693324].
- Self-improvement in the harness: Google Research's RRSI improved 6 of 6 held-out benchmarks with 30% fewer tokens [feed:memory 2026-09-29 https://x.com/Marktechpost/status/2104861598657785956].
- New trends: "Memory failures made attributable to write, read or reasoning" and "Memory compliance enforced outside the model" [feed:memory 2026-09-30].
